# P8.18b evidence: fixes from the sample acceptance

**Not tested on hardware (HC-4).** Acceptance rests on build evidence: configure, Verify with west, Apply to copies, full `west build` and
`__device_dts_ord_*` symbols in the linked image. Nothing was flashed or run on a board.

Environment: `DTX_RTOS4_WS=C:\Masters\26020_RTOS4\zephyrproject` (Zephyr v4.3.0, old `atmel` port, board `same54_xpro`),
`DTX_MAIN_WS=C:\Masters\zephyr-main` (Zephyr 4.5.0-rc1, board `pic32cx_sg41_cult`), `DTX_DESIGN_FILES=C:\Masters\board_design_files`,
Zephyr SDK 0.17.4. All work in `%TEMP%\dtx_p818b` (own copies of the three samples; the workspaces were not written into). The configure
method is the same as in P8.18: core APIs that the dialogs call (`ConfigSession`, `build_node`, the recipes, `board_nodes.apply_spec`,
`generic.build_child`, `verify.verify`, `apply.draft_plan/apply`, `port_report.port_build`, `apply.plan_apply/apply`).

## Summary

| item | what changed | test that fails without it |
|---|---|---|
| B2 | `board_nodes.pwm_controllers(model, draft)` also offers the nodes of class `pwm` that the draft holds (cell count from their `#pwm-cells`); `check_spec(..., draft=)` uses it; the dialog passes the session draft | `test_pwm_controllers_include_the_pwm_nodes_of_the_draft`, `test_pwm_led_on_a_controller_that_the_draft_enables_is_accepted`, UI `test_pwm_led_on_a_controller_the_draft_enables_and_zephyr_user` |
| B3 | `port_report._parse_items` no longer treats `&{/path}` as a block start (a `{` after `&`); `&{/} { }` is walked like `/ { }`; the root `status` is no decision; alias and chosen string paths (`"/user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool"`) must exist in the target or be defined by carried text | `test_parse_items_understands_a_path_reference_block`, `test_root_path_block_of_the_app_overlay_is_carried_like_a_root_block`, `test_alias_to_a_path_that_a_dropped_node_defines_is_dropped_too` |
| L1 | `BoardSpec.user` (`zephyr,user` with phandle-array properties of any name: controller label + cells), rendered as `zephyr,user { io-channels = <&adc1 6>, <&adc1 7>; };`, validated (name, controller in build or draft, cells >= 0), round trip through the draft file, Board nodes dialog section (ids `bn-user-*`) | `test_zephyr_user_overlay_text_round_trip_and_summary`, `test_zephyr_user_problems`, `test_zephyr_user_validates_on_the_real_build`, UI test above |
| port scope | `port_build(..., include_board_config=False)`: a node whose written properties all come from the source BOARD file is not ported (`[not_ported]` in section 1, or an `Unported` row in section 5 for nodes no recipe lifts), a node the app also touches is ported without the board file's properties, pads, chosen entries and aliases; pads used only by such nodes are not listed in section 4. `True` = the previous behaviour. Dialog checkbox `port-include-board` (off), status line counts them | `test_source_board_only_nodes_are_not_ported_by_default`, `test_covered_node_that_only_the_board_configures_is_not_ported_unless_asked`, `test_node_that_the_app_also_touches_is_ported_without_the_board_file_properties`, `test_pads_only_board_nodes_use_are_not_reported_as_pin_issues_by_default`, UI `test_port_include_board_configuration_checkbox` |
| clocks | the translation's clock rule text (`... Add in the target board file: &clock { gclkperiph { ... }; mclkperiph { ... }; };`) is emitted as an overlay piece when it is one balanced `&label { ... };` block and the target has that label; the note then reads `Emitted in the overlay: ...`; otherwise the report line stays and a note says why | `test_clock_rule_of_the_translation_becomes_an_overlay_piece`, `test_clock_rule_that_cannot_be_emitted_stays_a_report_line_with_the_reason` |
| found while re-running (not in the card list, same files) | the consumer cells (`pwms` third cell) of a node that the port newly enables were not rewritten: the target node is disabled with another compatible (`microchip,tcc-g1`), so its edtlib binding has no `pwm` specifier space. The cells now come from the binding YAML of the compatible the port sets (`_binding_cells`). Without it the SG41 fast check says `edtlib: missing data after phandle in <Property 'pwms' on /user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool ...>` | `test_consumer_cells_come_from_the_binding_of_the_compatible_the_port_sets` |

Existing tests whose result changed on purpose (default changed): `test_enabled_soc_node_without_recipe_is_listed_as_untranslatable`,
`test_enabled_soc_node_with_a_translation_but_no_recipe_is_listed_as_not_ported`, `test_lab1_to_sg41_new_pairs_are_translated_in_the_real_port`
(`port_build` with `include_board_config=True`, they test board configuration) and the UI test `test_port_lab1_to_e54_report` (ticks the checkbox:
lab1 is all source-board configuration). Nothing else changed its result.

Tests without the fixes (source files stashed, tests kept; `git stash push -- dt_explorer/core/port_report.py dt_explorer/core/board_nodes.py dt_explorer/ui/port.py dt_explorer/ui/board_nodes.py`, before the consumer-cells fix existed):

## Re-run of the three samples (SAME54 configured copies, SG41 cmake-only builds of the originals)

| sample | SAME54 configure (tool, no hand edit) | SG41 overlay contains | verify rc (same54 / sg41) | full build same54 / sg41 (FLASH) | in the image |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `basic/blinky_pwm` | `tcc3` PWM WO0 = PB16 (prescaler `<1>`, B1 fixed), Board nodes: PWM LED `pwm_led_tool` on `tcc3` ch 0 (B2: allowed because the draft enables tcc3), alias `pwm-led0` | only `tcc3` (pinctrl group, node with `#pwm-cells = <3>`, `compatible`), the `&clock` gclkperiph/mclkperiph entries of tcc3, the carried `/user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool` (B3, with the target's third cell `<&tcc3 0 20000000 0>`) and `&{/aliases} { pwm-led0 = "/user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool"; }`. No tcc0, gmac, mdio, adc0 or sercom node, no Ethernet `.conf` lines | 0 / 0 | rc 0 (23324 B) / rc 0 (32664 B; was 88540 B with the Ethernet lines) | tcc3 ord 86 / 114; `pwm-led0 -> /user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool` with `{'channel': 0, 'period': 20000000, 'polarity': 0}` on the SG41 |
| `drivers/adc/adc_dt` | `adc1` AIN6 = PB04, AIN7 = PB05 with `channel@6/7`; Board nodes `zephyr,user { io-channels = <&adc1 6>, <&adc1 7>; }` (L1) | `adc1` pad group and `channel@6/7` (the sample's own configuration), `/ { zephyr,user { io-channels } }` carried. No tcc0, gmac, mdio, adc0 | 0 / 0 | rc 0 (19608 B) / rc 0 (23868 B) | adc1 ord 9 / 12; `/zephyr,user io-channels` input 6 and 7 on both |
| `sensor/bme280` | `sercom5` I2C PA23/PA22, 100 kHz, child `bme280@76` | `sercom5` pad group, node with child `bme280_76`, the `&clock` entries of sercom5. No tcc0, gmac, mdio, adc | 0 / 0 | rc 0 (41020 B) / rc 0 (51540 B) | sercom5 ord 86 / 113, bme280_76 ord 87 / 114 |

The board-file configuration of the SAME54 (tcc0 pad override, gmac, mdio, adc0, sercom2/4/7, usb0, rtc, dac, gpio, `/leds`, `/pwmleds`, `/buttons`) appears in
section 1 as `[not_ported]` and in section 5 as `not ported (the target board's own applies)`; its children (`eeprom@5e`, `ethernet-phy@0`) in section 5b with the same wording.

## Notes and surprises

* The consumer-cells bug above showed up only because blinky_pwm now ports a *newly enabled* PWM controller together with its consumer (in P8.18 the consumer rewrite had only been exercised on `tcc0`, which the SG41 board enables). A first matrix run before that fix gave the same statuses (those rows use `tcc0`, enabled in the target).
* Clock entries: the text comes from the translation hook (`assigned_clock_to_gclkperiph`) as a one-line sentence ending in the `&clock { ... };` block; the port takes everything after `Add in the target board file: ` when it is a balanced `&label { ... };` and the label exists in the target (generic, no vendor name in `core`). The macros (`CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_TCC3`) resolve through the SoC dtsi includes: verify rc 0 and the link prove it, whether the peripheral really gets its clock is not tested (no hardware).
* Section 5 with the option off lists nodes such as `/cpus/cpu@0`, `/soc/rtc`, `/soc/dac`, the GPIO ports and the flash partitions as `not ported (the target board's own applies)` because the source board file writes properties into them. That is correct but longer than before; they were listed before too (as "carry it over by hand" or "translation exists, not ported").
* The first full-suite run had one failure outside the write scope: `tests/ui/test_ui_smoke.py::test_smoke_port` (browser console error `Anchor: target "#c109" not found`, from a Quasar tooltip I had put on the new checkbox with `.tooltip(...)`). Fixed in `ui/port.py` by using a `title` property like the other buttons; the smoke tests then passed (4 passed) and the suite was re-run in full (section 8).
* Vendor-term count in `core` (`grep -ciE "atmel|microchip|sam0|sercom|mchp"`): `core/port_report.py` 5 before / 5 after, `core/board_nodes.py` 1 / 1.
* Superseded first matrix run (before the consumer fix) is not pasted; its table equals the final one.


## 5. Scripts (all in `%TEMP%\dtx_p818b`, pasted in full; `cfg_i2c.py`, `nm.py`, `fb.cmd`, `build_s.cmd`, `build_m.cmd`, `explore.py` are the P8.18 scripts with the path `dtx_p818` replaced by `dtx_p818b`)


### common.py

```python
import sys, os, re, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, r"C:\Masters\dt_explorer")
from dt_explorer.app import load_plugins; load_plugins()
from dt_explorer.core import verify, apply as ap
from dt_explorer.core.build import BuildModel
from dt_explorer.ui import configure as C
from dt_explorer.ui.draft_tab import kconfig_lines
T = Path(os.environ["TEMP"]) / "dtx_p818b"

def show(findings):
    print("--- validate findings (%d) ---" % len(findings))
    for f in findings: print(f.severity, f.kind, f.node, f.message)
    errs = [f for f in findings if f.severity == "error"]
    print("errors:", errs); return errs

def do_verify(build, overlay, conf, tag):
    res = verify.verify(build, overlay, conf, on_line=None, scratch_root=T / ("verify_" + tag), timeout=900)
    print("--- Verify with west (%s) ---" % tag)
    print("rc:", res.returncode, "ok:", res.ok, "duration: %.1f s" % res.duration)
    print("errors:", res.errors); print("warnings:", res.warnings)
    print("cmd:", " ".join(res.cmd)); print("log tail:\n" + "\n".join(res.log_tail[-6:]))
    return res

def apply_to(build, app, overlay, conf):
    plan = ap.draft_plan(build, overlay, conf, app_dir=app)
    print("\n".join(ap.plan_lines(plan)))
    r = ap.apply(plan, append={"overlay", "conf"})
    print("written:", [str(x) for x in r.written], "skipped:", [str(x) for x in r.skipped], "refused:", [str(x) for x in r.refused])
    print("next:", "\n".join(ap.next_step_lines(plan)))
    return r

def port_apply(src_build, tgt_build, app):
    from dt_explorer.core.port_report import port_build
    res = port_build(src_build, tgt_build, include_board_config=bool(os.environ.get("INCLUDE_BOARD")))
    rep = res.report
    print("=== report ==="); print("\n".join(rep.lines()))
    print("\n=== overlay ===\n" + res.overlay); print("=== conf ===\n" + res.conf)
    import collections
    print("NODECOUNTS", dict(collections.Counter(x.status for x in rep.nodes)), "carried", len(rep.carried), "not_carried", len(rep.not_carried))
    bt, single = ap.target_of(tgt_build)
    plan = ap.plan_apply(app, bt, res.overlay, res.conf, single_soc=single, source_board=ap.target_of(src_build)[0])
    print("\n".join(ap.plan_lines(plan)))
    r = ap.apply(plan, append={"overlay", "conf"})
    print("written:", [str(x) for x in r.written], "skipped:", [str(x) for x in r.skipped], "refused:", [str(x) for x in r.refused])
    return res
```


### cfg_pwm.py

```python
from common import *
from dt_explorer.core import plugins, board_nodes as B
from dt_explorer.recipes import pwm_tc_tcc as P
build = T/"tgt_same54_blinky_pwm"; app = T/"same54"/"blinky_pwm"
PADS = {"WO0": os.environ.get("PWM_PAD", "PB18")}; LAB = os.environ.get("PWM_TCC", "tcc1")
m = BuildModel(build); s = C.ConfigSession(m); used = s.used_by()
print("build:", build, "| device:", s.device.name)
n = s.node_for_label(LAB); inst = s.instance_of(n)
compat = "atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm"; rec = plugins.get_recipe(compat)
opt = C.ClassOption("pwm", compat, rec); roles = C.option_roles(opt, inst)
props, problem = C.derive_props(opt, inst, PADS, roles); print("pads", PADS, "derived", props, "problem", problem)
node = C.build_node(option=opt, instance=inst, label=LAB, pins=PADS, roles_order=roles)
presc = P.suggest_prescaler(120e6, 20e6, 24); print("prescaler", presc)
node.vendor_props = rec.default_props(compat, inst.name, prescaler=presc)
s.upsert(node)
# Board nodes: PWM LED + alias pwm-led0 (core API of ui/board_nodes dialog)
spec = B.BoardSpec(entries=[B.Entry(kind=B.PWM_LEDS, name="pwm_led_tool", ctrl=LAB, channel=0, period_ns=20_000_000)],
                   aliases={"pwm-led0": "/user_pwm_leds/pwm_led_tool"})
pwm = B.pwm_controllers(m, s.draft); print("pwm controllers the dialog offers:", [p["label"] for p in pwm])
probs = B.check_spec(spec, model=m, pad_map=s.gpio_map(), pwm_ctrls={p["label"]: p for p in pwm},
                     existing_aliases={a for a in m.edt._dt.alias2node}, draft=s.draft)
print("check_spec problems:", [str(p) for p in probs])
B.apply_spec(s.draft, spec, s.gpio_map(), {p["label"]: len(p["cells"]) for p in pwm})
overlay = s.overlay(); print("\n--- overlay ---\n" + overlay)
show(s.check())
conf = "\n".join(kconfig_lines(s.kconfig())) + "\n"; print("--- conf ---\n" + conf)
r = do_verify(build, overlay, conf, "pwm")
if r.returncode == 0 and os.environ.get("APPLY"):
    apply_to(build, app, overlay, conf)
```


### cfg_adc.py

```python
from common import *
from dt_explorer.core import plugins, board_nodes as B
from dt_explorer.recipes import adc as A
build = T/"tgt_same54_adc_dt"; app = T/"same54"/"adc_dt"
m = BuildModel(build); s = C.ConfigSession(m); used = s.used_by()
print("build:", build, "| device:", s.device.name)
n = s.node_for_label("adc1"); inst = s.instance_of(n)
compat = A.OLD_COMPATIBLE; rec = plugins.get_recipe(compat)
opt = C.ClassOption("adc", compat, rec); roles = C.option_roles(opt, inst)
chosen = {"AIN6": "PB04", "AIN7": "PB05"}
props, problem = C.derive_props(opt, inst, chosen, roles); print("pads", chosen, "derived", props, "problem", problem)
node = C.build_node(option=opt, instance=inst, label="adc1", pins=chosen, roles_order=roles)
node.vendor_props = rec.default_props(compat, A.DEFAULT_GCLK_HZ)
node.children = A.channels_for(node.pins, reference="ADC_REF_INTERNAL", vref_mv=None)
print("children:", [(c.node_name, c.props) for c in node.children]); print("channel_problems:", A.channel_problems(node))
s.upsert(node)
# Board nodes: zephyr,user io-channels (L1), no hand edit
spec = B.BoardSpec(user={"io-channels": [("adc1", (6,)), ("adc1", (7,))]})
print("check_spec problems:", [str(p) for p in B.check_spec(spec, model=m, pad_map=s.gpio_map(), draft=s.draft)])
B.apply_spec(s.draft, spec, s.gpio_map())
overlay = s.overlay(); print("\n--- overlay ---\n" + overlay)
show(s.check())
conf = "\n".join(kconfig_lines(s.kconfig())) + "\n"; print("--- conf ---\n" + conf)
r = do_verify(build, overlay, conf, "adc")
if r.returncode == 0 and os.environ.get("APPLY"):
    apply_to(build, app, overlay, conf)
```


### cfg_i2c.py

```python
from common import *
from dt_explorer.core import generic as G
build = T/"tgt_same54_bme280"; app = T/"same54"/"bme280"
m = BuildModel(build); s = C.ConfigSession(m)
print("build:", build, "| device:", s.device.name)
n = s.node_for_label("sercom5"); inst = s.instance_of(n); print("sercom1 status", n.status)
opt = next(o for o in s.options_for(n) if o.cls == "i2c"); roles = C.option_roles(opt, inst)
pins = {"SDA": "PA23", "SCL": "PA22"}
props, problem = C.derive_props(opt, inst, pins, roles); print("pads", pins, "derived", props, "problem", problem)
bme = G.build_child(bus="i2c", compatible="bosch,bme280", address=G.parse_address("i2c", "76"))
print("child:", bme.node_name, bme.labels, bme.compatible, bme.props)
node = C.build_node(option=opt, instance=inst, label="sercom5", pins=pins, roles_order=roles, props={"clock-frequency": 100000}, children=[bme])
s.upsert(node)
overlay = s.overlay(); print("\n--- overlay ---\n" + overlay)
show(s.check())
conf = "\n".join(kconfig_lines(s.kconfig())) + "\n"; print("--- conf ---\n" + conf)
r = do_verify(build, overlay, conf, "i2c")
if r.returncode == 0 and os.environ.get("APPLY"):
    apply_to(build, app, overlay, conf)
```

### matrix.py

```python
import sys, os, time, collections
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, r"C:\Masters\dt_explorer")
from dt_explorer.app import load_plugins; load_plugins()
from dt_explorer.core import verify
from dt_explorer.core.port_report import port_build
TMP = Path(os.environ["TEMP"]); WS = Path(os.environ["DTX_RTOS4_WS"])
runs = [(f"lab{n}", f"sg{s}") for n in range(4) for s in (41, 61)] + [("lab1", "e54")]
only = sys.argv[1:]
rows = []
for lab, tg in runs:
    if only and f"{lab}:{tg}" not in only: continue
    src = WS / "apps" / lab / "build_same54_xpro"
    tgt = TMP / "dtx_lab1_e54" if tg == "e54" else TMP / f"dtx_p62_{lab}_pic32cx_{tg}_cult"
    t0 = time.time()
    res = port_build(src, tgt, check=True, include_board_config=True)
    print(f"=== {lab} -> {tg}: port_build {time.time()-t0:.1f} s (include_board_config=True) ===")
    print("--- target overlay ---\n" + res.overlay + "--- target conf ---\n" + res.conf)
    print("--- report ---\n" + "\n".join(res.report.lines()))
    st = "; ".join(f"&{n.label}={n.status}" for n in res.report.nodes)
    cons = "; ".join(f"{c.path} {c.prop}={c.status}" for c in res.report.consumers) or "none"
    r = verify.verify(tgt, res.overlay, res.conf, on_line=None, scratch_root=TMP / "dtx_p818b" / f"verify_{lab}_{tg}", timeout=900)
    print(f"--- verify rc {r.returncode} ok {r.ok} {r.duration:.0f} s; errors {r.errors}; warnings {len(r.warnings)} ---")
    rows.append((lab, tg, st, cons, r.returncode, r.duration))
print("\nMATRIX")
for lab, tg, st, cons, rc, d in rows:
    print(f"| {lab} | {tg} | {st} | {cons} | {rc} ({d:.0f} s) |")
```


### nm.py

```python
import sys, re, subprocess
from common import *
rows = [("blinky_pwm", "same54", ["tcc3", "pwm_led_tool", "tcc0", "pwm_led0"]), ("adc_dt", "same54", ["adc1"]), ("bme280", "same54", ["sercom5", "bme280_76"]),
        ("blinky_pwm", "sg41", ["tcc3", "pwm_led0", "tcc0"]), ("adc_dt", "sg41", ["adc1"]), ("bme280", "sg41", ["sercom5", "bme280_76"])]
for sample, board, labels in rows:
    b = T / f"full_{board}_{sample}"
    m = BuildModel(b)
    cache = (b / "CMakeCache.txt").read_text(errors="replace")
    nm = re.search(r"^CMAKE_NM:\w+=(.*)$", cache, re.M).group(1).strip()
    out = subprocess.run([nm, str(b / "zephyr" / "zephyr.elf")], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    present = set(int(x) for x in re.findall(r"__device_dts_ord_(\d+)\b", out))
    print(f"== {sample} / {board}: {len(present)} __device_dts_ord_* symbols; elf {(b/'zephyr'/'zephyr.elf').stat().st_size} B")
    byl = {}
    for n in m.edt.nodes:
        for lb in n.labels: byl[lb] = n
        byl[n.name] = n
    for lb in labels:
        n = byl.get(lb)
        if n is None: print(f"   {lb}: not in the devicetree"); continue
        print(f"   {lb}: {n.path} status={n.status} dep_ordinal={n.dep_ordinal} in image: {n.dep_ordinal in present}")
```


### chk.py

```python
import sys, os
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, r"C:\Masters\dt_explorer")
from dt_explorer.app import load_plugins; load_plugins()
from dt_explorer.core.build import BuildModel
T = Path(os.environ["TEMP"]) / "dtx_p818b"
for b in ("full_same54_blinky_pwm", "full_sg41_blinky_pwm"):
    m = BuildModel(T / b)
    a = m.edt._dt.alias2node
    print(b, "pwm-led0 ->", a["pwm-led0"].path, "| pwms:", m.by_path[a["pwm-led0"].path].props["pwms"].val[0].data)
for b in ("full_same54_adc_dt", "full_sg41_adc_dt"):
    m = BuildModel(T / b)
    print(b, "/zephyr,user io-channels:", [(c.controller.name, c.data) for c in m.by_path["/zephyr,user"].props["io-channels"].val])
```

	#io-channel-cells = <1>;
	#address-cells = <1>;
	#size-cells = <0>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&adc1_adc_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";

	channel@6 {
		reg = <0x6>;
		zephyr,gain = "ADC_GAIN_1";
		zephyr,reference = "ADC_REF_INTERNAL";
		zephyr,acquisition-time = <0>;
		zephyr,input-positive = <6>;
		zephyr,resolution = <12>;
	};

	channel@7 {
		reg = <0x7>;
		zephyr,gain = "ADC_GAIN_1";
		zephyr,reference = "ADC_REF_INTERNAL";
		zephyr,acquisition-time = <0>;
		zephyr,input-positive = <7>;
		zephyr,resolution = <12>;
	};
};

&{/} {
	status = "okay";

	zephyr,user {
		io-channels = <&adc1 6>, <&adc1 7>;
	};
};
/* dt_explorer end */

=== lab0 -> sg41: port_build 22.2 s (include_board_config=True) ===
--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg41_cult/pic32cx1025sg41128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg41_cult.overlay */

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* carried from apps\lab0\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:3 */
&{/aliases} {
	button0 = &button0;
};

/* carried from apps\lab0\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:8 */
&button0 {
	status = "okay";
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};

--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg61_cult/pic32cx1025sg61128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg61_cult.overlay */

/* &sercom2: atmel,sam0-uart -> microchip,sercom-g1-uart */
&pinctrl {
	sercom2_uart_default: sercom2_uart_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PB25D_SERCOM2_PAD0>,
				 <PB24D_SERCOM2_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom2 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-uart";
	current-speed = <115200>;
	rxpo = <1>;
	txpo = <0>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom2_uart_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/ {
	chosen {
		zephyr,console = &sercom2;
		zephyr,shell-uart = &sercom2;
	};
};

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &sercom2: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM2_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM2>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* carried from apps\lab0\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:3 */
&{/aliases} {
	button0 = &button0;
};

/* carried from apps\lab0\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:8 */
&button0 {
	status = "okay";
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};
--- target conf ---

CONFIG_ADC_MCHP_G1=y

CONFIG_NET_L2_ETHERNET=y
CONFIG_ETH_DRIVER=y
CONFIG_ETH_MCHP_GMAC_G1=y
CONFIG_MDIO_MCHP_GMAC_G1=y

=== lab1 -> sg61: port_build 59.1 s (include_board_config=True) ===
--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg61_cult/pic32cx1025sg61128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg61_cult.overlay */

/* &sercom2: atmel,sam0-uart -> microchip,sercom-g1-uart */
&pinctrl {
	sercom2_uart_default: sercom2_uart_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PB25D_SERCOM2_PAD0>,
				 <PB24D_SERCOM2_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom2 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-uart";
	current-speed = <115200>;
	rxpo = <1>;
	txpo = <0>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom2_uart_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/ {
	chosen {
		zephyr,console = &sercom2;
		zephyr,shell-uart = &sercom2;
	};
};

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &sercom2: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM2_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM2>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* carried from apps\lab1\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:2 */
/ {
	my_led0: my_led@41008000 {
	    compatible = "myvendor,mygpio-led";
	    reg = <0x41008000 0x2000>;
	    port = <2>;
	    pin = <18>;
	    status = "okay";
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};

CONFIG_NET_L2_ETHERNET=y

CONFIG_MDIO_MCHP_GMAC_G1=y

/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg41_cult/pic32cx1025sg41128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg41_cult.overlay */

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* carried from apps\lab2\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:2 */
/ {
	my_led0: my_led@41008000 {
	    compatible = "myvendor,mygpio-led";
	    reg = <0x41008000 0x2000>;
	    port = <2>;
	    pin = <18>;
	    status = "okay";
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};

--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg61_cult/pic32cx1025sg61128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg61_cult.overlay */

/* &sercom2: atmel,sam0-uart -> microchip,sercom-g1-uart */
&pinctrl {
	sercom2_uart_default: sercom2_uart_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PB25D_SERCOM2_PAD0>,
				 <PB24D_SERCOM2_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom2 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-uart";
	current-speed = <115200>;
	rxpo = <1>;
	txpo = <0>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom2_uart_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/ {
	chosen {
		zephyr,console = &sercom2;
		zephyr,shell-uart = &sercom2;
	};
};

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &sercom2: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM2_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM2>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* carried from apps\lab2\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:2 */
/ {
	my_led0: my_led@41008000 {
	    compatible = "myvendor,mygpio-led";
	    reg = <0x41008000 0x2000>;
	    port = <2>;
	    pin = <18>;
	    status = "okay";
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};
--- target conf ---

CONFIG_ADC_MCHP_G1=y

CONFIG_PWM_MCHP_G1_TCC=y
CONFIG_NETWORKING=y
CONFIG_NET_L2_ETHERNET=y
CONFIG_ETH_DRIVER=y
CONFIG_ETH_MCHP_GMAC_G1=y
CONFIG_MDIO_MCHP_GMAC_G1=y

--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg41_cult/pic32cx1025sg41128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg41_cult.overlay */

/* &sercom3: atmel,sam0-i2c -> microchip,sercom-g1-i2c */
&pinctrl {
	sercom3_i2c_default: sercom3_i2c_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PA22C_SERCOM3_PAD0>,
				 <PA23C_SERCOM3_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom3 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-i2c";
	#address-cells = <1>;
	#size-cells = <0>;
	clock-frequency = <400000>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom3_i2c_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";

	mytemp0: mytemp@4f {
		compatible = "mycompany,my-temp-sensor";
		reg = <0x4f>;
		temp-reg = <0>;
		resolution-bits = <9>;
		status = "okay";
	};
};

/ {
	aliases {
		sercom3 = &sercom3;
	};
};

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &sercom3: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom3 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM3_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom3 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM3>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* carried from apps\lab3\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:42 */
/ {
	my_http0: my_http0 {
	  compatible = "mycompany,my-http-temp";
	  status = "okay";
	  port = <8080>;
	  concurrent = <1>;
	  backlog = <10>;
	  temp-sensor = <&mytemp0>;
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};

--- target overlay ---
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg61_cult/pic32cx1025sg61128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg61_cult.overlay */

/* &sercom2: atmel,sam0-uart -> microchip,sercom-g1-uart */
&pinctrl {
	sercom2_uart_default: sercom2_uart_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PB25D_SERCOM2_PAD0>,
				 <PB24D_SERCOM2_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom2 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-uart";
	current-speed = <115200>;
	rxpo = <1>;
	txpo = <0>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom2_uart_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/ {
	chosen {
		zephyr,console = &sercom2;
		zephyr,shell-uart = &sercom2;
	};
};

/* &sercom3: atmel,sam0-i2c -> microchip,sercom-g1-i2c */
&pinctrl {
	sercom3_i2c_default: sercom3_i2c_default {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PA22C_SERCOM3_PAD0>,
				 <PA23C_SERCOM3_PAD1>;
		};
	};
};

&sercom3 {
	status = "okay";
	compatible = "microchip,sercom-g1-i2c";
	#address-cells = <1>;
	#size-cells = <0>;
	clock-frequency = <400000>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&sercom3_i2c_default>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";

	mytemp0: mytemp@4f {
		compatible = "mycompany,my-temp-sensor";
		reg = <0x4f>;
		temp-reg = <0>;
		resolution-bits = <9>;
		status = "okay";
	};
};

/ {
	aliases {
		sercom3 = &sercom3;
	};
};

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &sercom2: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM2_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom2 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM2>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* &sercom3: clock controller entries (from the translation's clock rule) */
&clock {
	gclkperiph {
		sercom3 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_GCLKPERIPH_ID_SERCOM3_CORE>;
			gclkperiph-src = "gclk0";
			gclkperiph-en = <1>;
		};
	};
	mclkperiph {
		sercom3 {
			subsystem = <CLOCK_MCHP_MCLKPERIPH_ID_APBB_SERCOM3>;
			mclk-en = <1>;
		};
	};
};

/* carried from apps\lab3\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:42 */
/ {
	my_http0: my_http0 {
	  compatible = "mycompany,my-http-temp";
	  status = "okay";
	  port = <8080>;
	  concurrent = <1>;
	  backlog = <10>;
	  temp-sensor = <&mytemp0>;
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};

=== overlay ===
/* Port of build_same54_xpro (same54_xpro/same54p20a) to pic32cx_sg41_cult/pic32cx1025sg41128
 * generated by Devicetree Explorer; apply as boards/pic32cx_sg41_cult.overlay */

/* &tcc0: atmel,sam0-tcc-pwm -> microchip,tcc-g1-pwm */
&pinctrl {
	tcc0_pwm_edit: tcc0_pwm_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC18F_TCC0_WO2>;
		};
	};
};

&tcc0 {
	#pwm-cells = <3>;
	prescaler = <8>;
	pinctrl-0 = <&tcc0_pwm_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* &gmac: atmel,sam0-gmac -> microchip,gmac-g1-eth */
&gmac {
	phy-handle = <&phy>;
};

/* &mdio: atmel,sam-mdio -> microchip,gmac-g1-mdio */
&pinctrl {
	mdio_mdio_edit: mdio_mdio_edit {
		group1 {
			pinmux = <PC11L_GMAC_GMDC>,
				 <PC12L_GMAC_GMDIO>;
		};
	};
};

&mdio {
	pinctrl-0 = <&mdio_mdio_edit>;
	pinctrl-names = "default";
};

/* carried from apps\lab1\boards\same54_xpro.overlay:2 */
/ {
	my_led0: my_led@41008000 {
	    compatible = "myvendor,mygpio-led";
	    reg = <0x41008000 0x2000>;
	    port = <2>;
	    pin = <18>;
	    status = "okay";
	};
};

/* /pwmleds/pwm_led_0: pwms with the target's cells */
&pwm_led0 {
	pwms = <&tcc0 2 20000000 0>;
};
